What drove it: `load/worlds.rs` 490 lines (about 150 of them tests), refcore's text scans `declared_ids` and `declared_asks` included; `ask/worlds.rs` 179; `load/names.rs` 164, of which about 110 moved from `steps.rs`.

## 2026-11-04 M0, G4a: types and units, declared; every row's value checked at load; bound variables (issue #56)

- **Built:**
  - `eval/read.rs`: the row converter is now refcore's `convert_value`, form by form and in its order: holes, `none`, `never`, literals, text, windows (`LOAD-DOMAIN window(a, b) with a after b`), date lists, records (a leg, an unknown field, a field twice, a missing field), calendars, distributions, expressions. Numbers: `LOAD-PRECISION` for a fraction that terminates (`1/2 terminates; write it as a decimal`) and for money finer than its minor unit; an int that isn't one; rates; years; keys; a `$` unit takes the row's keys (`gives one unit for keys of several units`). Code words and `nominal` as refcore checks them; an invalid month-day is `LOAD-PARSE`. Load and the read call the one converter (`ConvertFor`: the input's unit factor and the row's keys), so load refuses what a read would.
  - `load/values.rs`, refcore's `check_rows` and `place_value`: every row converted before any ask, in refcore's order (inputs, the balance readings by their place's unit, `any` places by the unit their row states, then defs' givens). A refusal is placed at its row's line. A form the runtime doesn't build yet is left to the read, never guessed.
  - `load/decls.rs`, refcore's `build_kind_types`: attribute types, an event kind's leg units and a place kind's unit, each resolved at load (`LOAD-NAME unknown id u (the unit of leg l of k)`, `LOAD-UNIT money in an undeclared currency`).
  - `load/binds.rs`, the declared half of refcore's `bind`: a bound variable named like a top-level id or a variable in scope (`LOAD-SHADOW`); what it ranges over (an index, a kind, observed movements, a key list, a range; `LOAD-NAME` unknown, `LOAD-KEY` not a domain, `LOAD-DOMAIN` an axis); a key list's and a kind's key spelling; `on`/`over` on things and movements (`LOAD-ALIVE`, and `LOAD-DOMAIN` on anything else).
  - `load/graph.rs`: the walk now knows where it stands. Each scope carries its file and line, and `Files` maps every item to its file, so an unknown id in an expression is `LOAD-NAME unknown id x (file:line)` at refcore's line. Moves are walked clause by clause at each clause's line. `prices … by` is refcore's `check_prices`: an unknown id, not an input or a def keyed by one time index, no key for the price year.
  - `syntax`: a move's clause lines (`MoveLines`) and each leg's line, so the walk places what refcore places by token. `base/refusal.rs`: `placed`, a place only where the refusal has none.
- **Green ids (4 new):** `m0_code_load_alive`, `m0_code_load_name`, `m0_code_load_precision`, `m0_code_load_shadow`. ALIVE (plan: G4b) and NAME's expression forms (plan: G4b) are whole here, beyond the plan.
- **Not green, and why:**
  - `m0_code_load_unit`: `basic/unit-mixed` (`num PLN and num have different units`) and `basic/unit-fx` (an exchange rate on the wrong currency) are the unit algebra in expressions, G4b's `types.rs`. `basic/unit-mode` is `round`'s modes, G4b's `calls.rs`. `adjust/unit-settlement` is G4c's. The declared forms (legs, kinds, rows, places) refuse as refcore does.
  - `m0_code_load_domain`: `basic/domain` (`range of quantities 0 PLN .. amt has no step`) is a range typed inside an expression, refcore's `t_*`, so it goes with G4b's typer. `basic/domain-discrete` stops "not implemented" (a discrete distribution). The declared forms (a window with a after b, a binding over an axis, `on`/`over` on keys) are built.
  - The milestone's red vectors: 345 stop "not implemented", and 37 print the probe's `REQ-ASK` where a later group's check refuses. One prints another refusal: G3's `basic/key-id` (G5's `check_key_ids`). No `ENGINE-INVARIANT`, no panic. No earlier green id was lost.
- **Deviations from the plan:**
  - Moved to G4b, since each is typed inside an expression by refcore's checker: a range with no step; a day key where a date is expected (2.1-5); rates and the unit algebra in expressions; the typed checks of a binding's `on`, `over` and `where`; `LOAD-COVERED`; the `LOAD-PARSE` forms of a read with keys.
  - Price clauses are walked but refuse nothing until G4c's priced places.
  - Moves are walked in the order they were declared (refcore's), where S walked them by id. The first of two faulty moves now refuses as in refcore.
  - At runtime, a `$` unit now takes the row's keys (it didn't), and a distribution value for a number type is now "not built" (it was misread as a number). Neither changes a green vector.
- **Own tests (not gating):** `tests/load_cases.rs`, `rows_kinds_prices_and_bindings_are_checked_at_load_as_refcore_checks_them`. These are 24 edits of one invented base text, each expected line copied from refcore's run of the same text: precision, fractions, ints, a month for a date, a reading's text, leg units, attribute types, `prices … by`, every binding refusal, an unknown id in a leg and a `when`. Inline in `load/binds.rs`: shadowing, domains, `on`/`over`.
- **No mutants-local:** no kernel file changed.
- **Totals:** `milestone-report: M0: 61/115 green` (from 57). No STOP-REPORT open. Exception delta: +0. Report-only hits are 5, as before: the explicit lifetimes in `ConvertFor` and `row_file`, and a nested closure in `Files::at_input`, were restructured away (owned fields, early returns). check full: "check full: green, 31 steps (896s)".
- **Size:** 15 files, +1 534 −217. Of that, 328 lines are tests (186 in `load_cases.rs`, 142 inline in `binds.rs`), so code is +1 206 −217 against the plan's 1 000 for G4a. What drove it:
  - the faithful `convert_value` twin, `eval/read.rs` +416 −144: every value form with refcore's message and order, shared by load and the read;
  - the walk's places, `load/graph.rs` +254 −55 and the syntax's clause lines +72: refcore names each token's line, so each refusal has to know its file and line;

## 2026-11-04 M0, G4b: types and units in expressions, the checker (issue #56)

- **Built:**
  - `load/check.rs` (was `graph.rs`; the edge recording kept, merged in): the checker's state (`Checker`: the graph, the four-digit figures waiting for a reading, the key lists' fixed indexes and their uses, each def's key offsets), `Scope` (where an expression stands for the literal rule, `allow_lit`, cited, in a `when`, the `covered()` spans) and `Var` (type, domain, key list, its own id), and the walk in refcore's order: price clauses, inputs' ranges, moves, defs, bands, asks, `prices … by`, then the year literals' literal rule and the key lists' readings. Moves: binds, `on` (a date) at its own node, `at` (`LOAD-LAWPERIOD`), `for`, `when` (a bool), the legs and attributes against the kind's (`LOAD-LEG`), each leg's places, amount (typed where the leg's unit is expected, then assigned) and day, `fulfils`, `ties by`, `reads`, the written order. Defs: key binds' ranges and `where`, `at`, `beats` (`LOAD-LABEL`, the beats cycle `LOAD-CYCLE` as refcore names it), each rule's `when` and value of the def's type. Bands: each row of the band's type, its argument in scope. Asks: binds, `law`, `as known`.
  - `load/types.rs`: refcore's `tc` node by node: literals and the literal rule (inline 0, 1, −1; figures and dates in a rule; figures in an ask), numbers with units, a currency's precision, four-digit figures (a year or an int, decided where refcore decides, their literal rule at the end), key literals resolved by position, names and reads (keys by position against each bind's domain, `$` units taking the read's keys, an observation read by its id), places and movement instances, postfixes and fields (`LOAD-FIELD`), records, lists, `-x`, the arithmetic chains.
  - `load/ops.rs`: arithmetic and the unit algebra (like units in a sum, a product's unit, exchange rates from B to A), date, period and key arithmetic, `LOAD-AMBIG`, `not`, `and`/`or`, `if` (with `covered()` guards into its branches), comparisons (a `pos` guard holds only an offset of the def's key arithmetic), key-list variables as values, membership, aggregates and `last`.
  - `load/calls.rs`: every built-in of refcore's `c_*` that M0 types: the date functions, calendar arguments (read at each day asked about), `round` (its step: 1 with a unit, `minor(CUR)` or a row in a rule; its mode a rounding code; money where the step is a whole number of minor units), `minor`, `abs`, `min`/`max`, `missing` (`LOAD-MISSING`), `since`, `band_lo`/`band_hi`, `marginal` (`LOAD-BAND` over a first row from −inf), band calls; `next`, `observed`, `gap`, `bal`, `flows`, `covered`, `value`, `leg … of`, `to` record their reads and marks as before and are typed `Unseen`.
  - `load/binds.rs`: the typed half of refcore's `bind` (what it ranges over and the variable's type, the reads of a binding over places, movements and observations, `on` a date, `over` a span, `where` a bool), `LOAD-COVERED` for a def's aggregate over observations, `range_type` (`LOAD-DOMAIN range of quantities … has no step`, a range of dates by a duration, ends and step of one unit), and `assign` (refcore's, arm by arm: key literals resolved where a key or a code is declared, a bare figure's precision for money, `round` needed for a num stored as money, keys of indexes and kinds, records' fields).
  - `model/ty.rs`: a bare figure carries its value (`the figure 2`, truncated as refcore's `%d` prints it); `KeyLit`, `KeyList`, `Dyn`, `UnitKey`, and `Unseen` for a form the checker types later, which no check refuses.
- **Green ids (9 new):** `m0_load_order_the_checks_past_parsing` (`order/items`, `items-control`, `passes-control`); per-code `m0_code_load_literal`, `_field`, `_missing`, `_covered`, and beyond the plan `_band`, `_label`, `_lawperiod`, `_leg`. Within red tests, these vectors are green too: `basic/key-count`, `basic/key-two` (LOAD-KEY), `basic/unit-mixed`, `basic/unit-fx` (C-16's FX fixture), `basic/domain` (a range with no step). `m0_code_load_ambig`, `_alive` and `_name` (with #45's binding over an undeclared name) stay green.

## STOP-REPORT 2026-11-04: check full's parse fuzz target runs out of memory loading this worktree's grown corpus, so G4b can't be committed green

- **Kind:** other (the fuzz step's local state; not G4b's code)
- **Where:** `engine/tools/check.py` `fuzz()`, the `parse` target; its corpus `engine/fuzz/corpus/parse/` (gitignored, grown by each check full run in this worktree, now 12 667 files, 33.7 MB).
- **Rule or gate:** R-91 (the fuzz step), R-77 (never commit red).
- **What happens:** `engine/tools/check full` → "check full: red, 1 of 31 steps failed (919s)", "FAIL fuzz: parse: the fuzzer found a crash, a hang or a failed assertion". fuzz.log: "seed corpus: files: 12667 … total: 33701841b", "#8192 pulse … rss: 311Mb", then "ERROR: libFuzzer: out-of-memory (used: 2073Mb; limit: 2048Mb)" before INITED, while reading its seeds. The run before, its corpus smaller, stopped the same way later in its 30 s ("rss: 2085Mb" at #16516; libFuzzer's live heap 49 MB, most of it its own corpus). Neither saved input reproduces: `tally reprint` takes 3.5 MB and 17 MB on them, identical on G4a's binary and this one; `tally::parse`, all the target calls, has no line changed by G4b. The `load` target, which runs the new checker, passes in the same runs. All 30 other steps are green.
- **What I tried:** moving the grown corpus aside (kept, so CI's seeds-only start would be met): the guard refuses (`engine/fuzz/corpus/parse`, protected and checksummed, R-80, R-81, R-91). Nothing else touched: no flag, no retry, no corpus edit.
- **What I ask:** options: **(a) Recommended:** the rules role (or the coordinator) resets or minimises this worktree's parse corpus (for example, back to `fuzz/seeds.py`'s seeds, as CI starts), and bounds its growth (a `cmin` before the run, or a fresh corpus per run), then the builder re-runs check full and commits G4b as it stands; (b) the coordinator lets G4b commit with check full red on this one step, CI's fresh-corpus run being the judge; (c) a raised `-rss_limit_mb` for the parse target (a rule change, R-112). The two saved inputs are in `tmp/m0/g4b/oom/` (`case.tally`, `case2.tally`).
- **State:** last green commit edc4c1f (G4a). Uncommitted: all of G4b, check fast green: the 13 files under `engine/crates` (only the rename `graph.rs` → `check.rs` staged; `calls.rs`, `ops.rs`, `types.rs` added as intent-to-add) and this file. The commit is ready to make as it stands once check full is green; notes for whoever makes it are in `tmp/m0/g4b/notes.md`.
- **Resolved:** #78, pick P2 (a), 03fd831. check's fuzz step now gives each target a fresh, seeds-only corpus (`target/fuzz-corpus/<target>/`), and this worktree's corpora are back to the seeds; the rules step's diagnosis is ASan's quarantine (the grown corpus peaks at 2 053 MB under ASan, 72 MB without it, per-input memory flat), nothing in the engine. With G4b as it stood, "check full: green, 31 steps (141s)"; G4b committed on top.

## 2026-11-04 M0, G4c: moves, legs and reads; priced places; the settlement's unit at load (issue #56)

- **Built:**
  - `load/moves.rs`, refcore's `m_check_leg` and the rest of `m_check_move`: each end of a leg typed where a place is expected (`leg l of m: to is T, not a place`), no leg to or from an asset, kind or thing (`LOAD-KIND`), one unit between its places, the leg's fixed unit against the unit they hold, a `Dyn` leg taking its places' unit, an amount of a unit kind's unit, then `assign` or a numeric check, the leg's own `on` a date. A move's `fulfils` names a movement; its `reads` a place or a move (`LOAD-READS … which is not a place or a move`). A decision reading `bal(x, d)` of its own day without `start`, or of a later day, without a projection: `LOAD-CYCLE … its own result or its future (write the projection)`. The leg's static type is kept for `flows` and the settlements.
  - The settlement's unit (`LOAD-UNIT settlement o of adjustments[m.l, V] is in U, not A`), at load after the walk, as #72 rules. refcore meets it only when a read settles the adjustment (moves.py's `settle_sign`); load checks every observed row that fulfils an adjustment against the static unit of the leg it settles, and prints it with no place, as refcore does.
  - `load/prices.rs`, refcore's `m_check_priced`, `m_resolve_price`, `m_t_value`, `_all_priced` and `model.price_of`'s refusal: a price input named on a kind and on its thing (`LOAD-PRICE … names a price input on its kind k and on itself`, before the walk, as refcore's model builds); each `priced` clause of a kind or a thing resolved and typed in refcore's order (unknown, not an input or a def, `last` of a def, a value or a price per unit, its keys with the section's binds, `LOAD-KEY`); an asset with no price input; `value(x, d) in CUR [by p]` with its branches (`LOAD-PRICE … holds units in U and states no by`, a whole value in another currency, an unkeyed or keyed price input or def, `last(p)`, an expression of `num CUR/u`, `LOAD-NAME unknown price input`). `value` is `num CUR`.
  - `load/reads.rs`, refcore's `m_place_arg` to `m_unit_of_quantity`: the types of `bal` (one or two arguments, a currency, the day a date, `start`, `modelled`, a projection's `from` a date, `of q`), `low` (`LOAD-DOMAIN` for an open span), `flows` of a place or a move or a move's leg (`LOAD-LEG`, `LOAD-KEY`, a place of unit `any` refused unless converted, `in CUR at fx mode`), `covered`, `gap`, `next` (an event kind), `observed` (a planned instance; its type the move's record), `unit` (`unit(x)` of a place or a quantity), `of q` (`sort_of`: a party, a batch, the pair; `LOAD-PARTS` for a literal place that keeps no labels of its sort) and the leg read `m.leg.amount of q` (`LOAD-FIELD`). ## STOP-REPORT 2026-11-05: the canonical form prints a prefixed call `a.f(x)` as `a.f (x)`, which no parser reads back; refcore prints the same bytes

- **Kind:** the spec seems wrong (refcore, the oracle, breaks L §2.3/§2.4; the engine twins it byte for byte)
- **Where:** engine `crates/tally/src/syntax/spacing.rs:68–70` (a `(` joins its name only when the name is a built-in or a token marked `name`), with the marks set in `syntax/parse.rs` and `syntax/expr.rs`; refcore `canon.py:344–349` (`line_text`, `p.nm`) and `parser.py:544–557` (`post`: the id after a `.` is read as a field and never marked `nm`, even when it becomes a call's name). L §2.3's call grammar, `ID {"." ID} "("`, reads a prefixed call only with no space before `(` (refcore: `not self.peek().ws`); L §2.4: the loader reprints the model.
- **Rule or gate:** R-91 (the `format` fuzz target: a canonical text reprints to itself); DONE gate 10 (refcore's bytes); L §2.3, §2.4.
- **What happens:** `engine/tools/check full` (G5's first run) → "check full: red, 3 of 31 steps failed (401s)", of them "FAIL fuzz: format: the fuzzer found a crash … (see fuzz/artifacts/format/)". fuzz.log: "panicked at fuzz_targets/format.rs:33:26: the canonical text is rejected: Refused(Refusal { code: Load(Parse), message: \"cannot parse: expected \\\")\\\", got (\" … })". The input, a seed with one byte changed (`year_of(m)` → `year_.f(m)`), is `fuzz/artifacts/format/crash-5eeeed0b18a782141ea5ff7c0f4779db913db8ae` (gitignored). The smallest: `def c: num = sum(n[k] for k in qs where year_.f(k) == 1)` (with a header, an index `qs` and an input `n`, `tmp/m0/g5/fmt/a.tally`): `tally reprint` and `python3 -m refcore reprint` both print `where year_.f (k) == 1`, one hash (`dc5d18ac…`); both then refuse that text, `LOAD-PARSE cannot parse: expected ")", got (` (`tmp/m0/g5/fmt/b.tally`), and `def d: num = year_.f (2)` is `… expected end of line, got (` (`c.tally`). A member's band call in a joint model, `household.b(x)`, has the same form. G5 changed nothing under `syntax/`; the defect is G2's printer's, as old as it. The second run, after G5's own fixes, was green: its fuzz step didn't meet the input in its 20 s.
- **What I tried:** nothing in the code. A fix only in the engine (mark the call's name as refcore's `ident` marks names, so `(` joins it) would make the engine print other bytes than refcore on every such text, against DONE gate 10, and refcore is fixed first (DONE rule 3). The regression input is the rules role's to add (`fuzz/regressions/format/`).
- **What I ask:** options: **(a) Recommended:** a 2.1.3 ruling that the canonical form prints a prefixed call's `(` joined to its name, as every call (L §2.3's grammar; L §2.4 "none inside brackets"). refcore's `parser.py` `post` marks the call's last name `nm`, as `ident` does (canon.py then joins it); the builder makes the twin change in `syntax/expr.rs` (`post`, where it calls `band_call`: one mark on the name's token), with an own test of the reprint; the rules role adds the input to `fuzz/regressions/format/`. (b) The same fix in the engine now, ahead of refcore, logged as a byte difference until refcore follows. (c) The `format` target keeps it as a known finding until joint models (M2) print member calls, refcore unchanged meanwhile (not recommended: every joint model with a member's band call would fail to reprint).
- **State:** last green commit: G5's, below this report in history (the commit that adds this file's G5 entry and this report). Nothing of the fix made; the engine prints refcore's bytes.

- **Built:**

The strata moved ahead of the arrival reads, as refcore's.

## STOP-REPORT 2026-11-05: the canonical form prints a prefixed call whose name is a keyword word, `mi.or(DUK)`, as `mi.or (DUK)`, which no parser reads back; refcore 2.1.4 prints the same bytes

- **Kind:** the spec seems right and refcore wrong (refcore 2.1.4, the oracle, breaks tally 2.1.4's L §2.4; the engine twins it byte for byte)
- **Where:** engine `crates/tally/src/syntax/spacing.rs:68` (`let call = p.kind == Kind::Id && (is_builtin(&p.text) || (!is_keyword(&p.text) && pm.name));`); refcore `canon.py:347` (`if p.t == 'ID' and (p.s in BUILTINS or (p.s not in KEYWORDS and p.nm))`). 2.1.4 marks a prefixed call's name (`nm`, the engine's `name`), but the keyword test before it still spaces a name that is a keyword word (`or`, `and`, …), which L §2.3 allows after a `.` ("A word right after `.` is a field, never a keyword").
- **Rule or gate:** R-91 (the `format` fuzz target: a canonical text reprints to itself); DONE gate 10 (refcore's bytes); L §2.3 parse rule 3, L §2.4 Expressions as 2.1.4-1 states it ("none before a call's `(`, its prefixes and a band's key included (`f(x)`, `a.b(x)`, `b[k](x)`)").
- **What happens:** `engine/tools/check full` (this step's second run, on item 1's code, which touches no file under `syntax/`) → "check full: red, 1 of 31 steps failed (501s)": "FAIL fuzz: format: the fuzzer found a crash, a hang or a failed assertion (see fuzz/artifacts/format/)". fuzz.log: "panicked at fuzz_targets/format.rs:33:26: the canonical text is rejected: Refused(Refusal { code: Load(Parse), message: \"cannot parse: expected \\\")\\\", got (\", place: Some(Line(\"input\", 6)) })". The input (`fuzz/artifacts/format/crash-2e3648fc92206b4f00433b133d01565ab13954f1`, untracked) is a mutated seed: SURF-04's `redefine` scenario with `minor(DUK)` turned into `mi.or(DUK)`. Smallest input: a model with `unit DUK: currency minor 0.01`, `input gross: money DUK` and `def contrib: money DUK = round(gross, mi.or(DUK), half-up)`: `tally reprint` and `python3 -m refcore reprint` both print `round(gross, mi.or (DUK), half-up)` (and `mi.and (DUK)` for `and`; `mi.min(DUK)` prints joined), and a reprint of that text is `refused: LOAD-PARSE cannot parse: expected ")", got ( (…:11)` on both.
- **What I tried:** nothing in the code. Dropping the keyword test for a token the parser marks a name (`is_builtin(&p.text) || pm.name`, refcore `p.s in BUILTINS or p.nm`) would print `mi.or(DUK)`, which reads back; done in the engine alone it prints other bytes than refcore on every such text (DONE gate 10), and refcore is fixed first (DONE rule 3), as for #80. ## 2026-11-05 M0, G8 (3 of 3, part 1): #75's load refusals: the base names no scenario item, a scenario's move before its `from`, a draw past its slots, a decision's projection reaching a random amount (issue #56)

- **Built (spec wins, #75; refcore makes none of these at load):**
  - `load/worlds.rs`: the base of a run with scenarios loads knowing the ids the scenarios' files declare, each with the first such scenario by id (`Reality.elsewhere`, refcore's `declared_ids`; G3's rename of the base's refusal now reads the same map). A name the base doesn't declare, read where it would be a key literal (not at a key or a code position), that a scenario's file declares and no key, option or thing of the base holds, is `LOAD-SCENARIO <x> of scenario <s> is read outside it` where it is typed (`read_elsewhere`, from `load/types.rs`): an ask's line (refcore refuses it at run as `LOAD-KEY ambiguous key`) and a plain read in arithmetic (refcore's `LOAD-UNIT … keylit in arithmetic`). A rule's or a `takes`' `v` naming a scenario's version is `LOAD-SCENARIO <item> of the base names <v> of scenario <s>` (`no_version`, at `load/decls.rs`, `rows.rs` and `parts.rs`; refcore's `LOAD-NAME … names no version`). Scenario worlds load with an empty map, as refcore renames only the base's refusal.
  - `load/across.rs` `check_scenario_moves`: a move a scenario's file declares, at each choice of its keys over declared indexes, whose `on` (a window's first day) or a leg's own day load sees fall before the scenario's `from` is `LOAD-SCENARIO move <m> of <s> books on <d>, before <from>`, at the move's or the leg's line, last in load.
  - `load/cone.rs` `check_draw_slots`, refcore's `m_check_draw_slots` after the cone: a slot key whose range ends at a random input or def (`load/types.rs` `key_pos` records it, `Var.end` keeps a range's end), at a key of an integer index, is `LOAD-RANDOM the draw <x> can exceed the last slot of <index>` when a row or rule can draw past the last key (`load/random.rs` `support_max`, refcore's `dist_support_max`, plus `discrete` over an integer index's keys).
  - `load/across.rs` `check_proj_seen`, refcore's `m_check_proj_seen` after `finish_random` (which now returns the cone): a decision's projection `bal(x, d) from t` outside a query (`load/reads.rs` records it, `ProjRead`), its day, t and d seen, and a random plan with no `when` booking x on a seen day in (t, d] whose leg amount reads a random input or def (no `if`, keys seen) at a law date after t: `LOAD-RANDOM <decision>[<keys>] on <day>: <projection> reaches random <x>[<keys>] after <t> with no random clause or query` at the projection's line. refcore skips a random move with no `at`; here its amount is dated by its day.

Each held gap fixture now prints the spec's form: `scen/scenario-read` (`LOAD-SCENARIO extra of scenario raised is read outside it (model.tally:18)`), `scen/scenario-names` (`… doubled of the base names raise_2027 of scenario raised (model.tally:15)`), `scen/scenario-move` (`scenario raised: refused: LOAD-SCENARIO move gift of raised books on 2026-06-01, before 2027-01-01 (scen.tally:23)`), `basic/random-projection`, `basic/random-slots`; `own/parts-no-total` was G6's. ## 2026-11-05 M0, G10 (1 of 3): a scenario's expression rows typed, and searched for what they read; a scenario's reality checks only the asks its world evaluates (issue #56)

- **Built** (`tmp/m0/g8s3-handoff.md` § Item 4, the last load check refcore makes that the engine didn't):
  - `load/srows.rs` (new), refcore's `m_check_scen_rows`, after the random rows: each expression row of a scenario's version (an input's row, a def's given) typed as a row of its target (`LOAD-UNIT a row of <x> …`). `<base>.x` reads x in the base (the model, or the scenario the row's is over) with `t_name`'s base-world checks: `LOAD-NAME unknown id <base>.<x>`, `LOAD-SCENARIO <base>.<x>: <x> is declared in scenario <s>, not in the base`, `LOAD-SCENARIO <base>.<x>: an expression row reads inputs, bands and defs, never a <sort>` (`load/types.rs` `base_read`); `<base>.b(x)` calls the base's band (`load/calls.rs`); a keyed `<base>.x[k]` names the base no more, as refcore's rewrite leaves it. The row's reads hang off its own node (`NodeKind::Srow`, refcore's `('srow', …)` item), which its input or def reads, so the graph's searches (unread items, axes, law dates, the random cone, which leaves it out as refcore's `ITEMS` does) meet it as refcore's do: a def read only by an expression row is read (`WARN-UNREAD`).
  - refcore's `m_check_srow_reads`, after the decisions' projections (`load/strata.rs`): from each row, through defs, fills and bands, a place, the balances, a move or an observed input's movements is `LOAD-SCENARIO the expression row of <x> in <s> reads <the place p | a balance | the move m | the movements of x>[ through a, b]: it reads inputs, bands and defs, never a place or a movement`, at the row's line. Each reader's reads are searched in the order it first made them (`Walked.read_order`, refcore's edges in insertion order), the last def reached first, as refcore's stack.
  - refcore's `top_scenario` and `ask_worlds` (`load/check.rs` `walk`, `load/strata.rs`): a scenario's reality checks, and keeps, only the asks whose line is evaluated in its top scenario: the ask's file's scenario, its `in`, a `diff`'s worlds, `with scenario` pins, `as if {scenario s}` in its line or in a def it reaches (the defs its line names, then the graph's edges). The others are the base's, or another scenario's.
  - Seen on the way, twinned (were "not built"): a call whose prefix names neither the base nor a member's band, `other.tax(x)`: `LOAD-NAME unknown id other.tax`, or `LOAD-NAME cannot parse: a.b names no band (a prefix is a member or the base)` (refcore's `prefixed_call`; a joint model's merge has made each member's band one name); a binding over a prefixed name, `for f in home.flat`: `LOAD-NAME unknown id flat` (refcore's `qualified`, which names nothing since GD-1).

The load, loadcheck and per-code vectors: 537 of 537 print refcore's bytes, as before (`tmp/m0/g10/vv-1.txt`).

## STOP-REPORT 2026-11-05: an index range `by 0` names no step; refcore reads it as `by 1`, the spec says nothing

- **Kind:** the spec is silent (no vector holds the form)
- **Where:** L §2.3's grammar, `index … ["by" INT]` (`spec/LANGUAGE.md:194`), and L §4.1's `by n` ("A key of a range `by n` stands for the n periods from it to the next key", `spec/LANGUAGE.md:562`): nothing says n ≥ 1; refcore `loader.py:626` and `:633` (`step = s.by or 1`, `n = s.by or 1`: a `by 0` is read as `by 1`); engine `crates/tally/src/load/indexes.rs:88` (`a range's by that isn't a positive integer`, not built).
- **Rule or gate:** DONE gate 10 (refcore's bytes); L's "Everything is explicit" (`spec/LANGUAGE.md:42`: "what a text doesn't state is refused or is a hole, never a default").
- **What happens:** an invented model (`tmp/m0/g10/nb.py`, cases `by-zero`, `by-zero-day`, `by-zero-int`): `index mon = month 2026-01..2026-03 by 0` (and a day range, an int range). `python3 -m refcore run a.tally --ask zz` → `refused: REQ-ASK no ask zz`, exit 1: refcore loads the index as `by 1`. The engine stops "not built", exit 69.
- **What I tried:** nothing in the code beyond leaving the form not built: reading 0 as 1 is a default no text states, and refusing it prints other bytes than refcore's; no vector holds either, and no green id waits on it.
- **What I ask:** options: **(a) Recommended:** the next spec round states that n is at least 1 (L §4.1) and gives the refusal (`LOAD-PARSE cannot parse: a step of 0` or a `LOAD-KEY` row's form, the round's choice); refcore refuses; T adds the case; the engine refuses with the spec's text (one arm in `load/indexes.rs`). **(b)** the spec states that `by 0` is `by 1`, refcore's reading; the engine follows. **(c)** the engine prints refcore's bytes now (0 read as 1), and the spec round settles it later.
- **State:** last green commit 9b4b162; this step's commit holds the form as "not built".

## STOP-REPORT 2026-11-05: three load refusals where refcore prints its parse tree, not the text: a band row or a `same` side keyed `*` (`LOAD-KEY star is not a key of flat`), and a `moves` line naming a keyed or prefixed version (`LOAD-MOVES moves index(obj=name(id='v1'), …)`)

- **Kind:** refcore seems wrong, the spec right (no vector holds the forms)
- **Where:** L §2.3's grammar: `bandrow = qid ["[" key "]"] …` (`spec/LANGUAGE.md:181`) and `sref = ID {"." FIELD | "[" lit {"," lit} "]"}` (`:298`) hold one key, or literals, where a row's `rowkeys` (`:232`) also take `*` and `a..b`; `{IN "moves" ref "to" lspan NL}` (`:167`) takes a `ref`. refcore's parser takes `*` and ranges in both places; `loader.py:1364` (`band_item` → `pattern_key`) refuses at `:2604` with `'%s is not a key of %s' % (t or kn.k, ix.id)`, the parse node's kind (`star`, `krange`) as the key, and `sref` the same; `loader.py:725` formats the `moves` ref's node with `%s`, its Python repr. Engine: `crates/tally/src/load/rows.rs` `band_item` (`a band key that isn't one key`), `load/same.rs` (`a reference keyed by * or a computed key`), `load/spans.rs` (`a moves line naming a member's or a keyed version`), each not built; the engine's parser takes the forms, as refcore's does.
- **Rule or gate:** DONE gate 10 (refcore's bytes); L §2.3, §5.3, §5.9, §6.1; L §8.3 (a message names the text).
- **What happens:** an invented model (`tmp/m0/g10/nb.py`): `band zb[f in flat](x: money PLN): money PLN` with the row `zb[*]: [0 PLN, inf) = 1.00 PLN` (case `band-key-range-ok`), or `zb[a1..a2]: …` (`band-key-range`); `same fee[*] = fee[a1]` (`same-star`); a second version with `moves v1[a1] to 2026-02-01..` (`moves-keyed`) or `moves a.v1 to …` (`moves-member`, no joint model). `python3 -m refcore run a.tally --ask zz`, exit 1: `refused: LOAD-KEY star is not a key of flat (a.tally:37)`; `LOAD-KEY krange is not a key of flat (a.tally:37)`; `LOAD-KEY star is not a key of flat (a.tally:37)`; `LOAD-MOVES moves index(obj=name(id='v1'), keys=[name(id='a1')]): unknown, or not known before this version (a.tally:39)`; `LOAD-MOVES moves field(obj=name(id='a'), name='v1'): unknown, or not known before this version (a.tally:39)`. The engine stops "not built" on each, exit 69.
- **What I tried:** nothing in the code beyond leaving the forms not built: refcore's bytes name its parser's internals, which no engine should copy; the grammar's reading refuses the first two at parse, which changes what the parser and the canonical form take, and prints other bytes than refcore's.
- **What I ask:** options: **(a) Recommended:** the next spec round fixes refcore, no spec text change: a band row's key and a `same` side's keys are refused at parse as the grammar says (`LOAD-PARSE cannot parse: expected a key, got *`, the round's wording), and a `moves` line prints its ref's text (`LOAD-MOVES moves v1[a1]: unknown, or not known before this version`); T adds the cases; the engine follows (its parser, and one message). **(b)** the spec widens the grammar (a band table for several keys, a `same` over every key) and says what each means; the engine builds it. **(c)** the engine prints refcore's bytes now, its node names included, and the spec round settles it later.
- **State:** last green commit 9b4b162; this step's commit holds the forms as "not built".

## 2026-11-05 M0 built: every "not built" stop M0 leaves, and who takes it (issue #56)

M0 loads and formats every text of L §2 and §8.3's catalogue, in refcore's order; the run past load is S's. A form the engine doesn't build stops the run with "not implemented" (exit 69), never a guess. This entry lists every such stop in `crates/tally/src/` at the end of M0 (`Stop::NotBuilt`, 185 sites: load 76, eval 58, walk 19, book 15, ask 11, read 3, syntax 3; by their texts), each with the milestone of `spec/BUILD-ORDER.md`, the issue or the STOP-REPORT that takes it. Scratch: `tmp/m0/g10/nb-inventory.txt` (the sites), `nb.py`, `jp.py` (the probes, refcore against the engine).

**Load, where refcore and the spec disagree (the next spec round; each a STOP-REPORT, the form left not built until it rules):**
- a move instance named by an observed attribute (`load/observed.rs`): #86, the coordinator's pick P10 (a), refcore's `check_mv_refs` fixed in the round;
- an index range `by 0` (`load/indexes.rs`): this commit's first STOP-REPORT;
- a band row keyed `*` or by a range (`load/rows.rs`), a `same` side keyed `*` (`load/same.rs`), a `moves` line naming a keyed or prefixed version (`load/spans.rs`): this commit's second STOP-REPORT (refcore prints its parse tree).

**Load, a twin not taken (refcore refuses; no vector holds the form):**
- an observed attribute written as an expression, `of_pay: 1 + 1` (`load/observed.rs`): refcore's `convert_value` refuses `LOAD-UNIT a value of the wrong type for bank[r2]` for an operator, and has arms of its own for records, lists, calendars and distributions; twinned whole with the observations at M2 (BUILD-ORDER M2, "Observations in full").
- two places stated `same` at their keys (`load/same.rs`, G9): M5c (joint members).
- refcore's `check_scenario_reads` is no stop but a check not built (G10 step 1): what it catches, a lower world's load refuses first. M1 (scenarios as overlays).

**Joint models' remaining load and format forms: M5c (joint members as contexts):**
- the canonical order of keys of a member's index or kind where two members' differ (`syntax/canon_order.rs`, G9);
- a member's pin, `with a.x = …` and a member's id in a wording pin (`load/pins.rs`, three sites);
- a joint model's files loaded without its members (`load/steps.rs`): an invariant of the order of the joint loader, which always hands the merge its members; M5c's contexts replace it.

**Load, forms of later milestones (refcore's own load stops at most of them, `NotYet`):** fills keyed over movements, a fill read inside its own supply (`load/fills.rs`): M5a; a projection's `random at` (`load/reads.rs`): M7.

**Capacity limits the spec doesn't state (refcore's Python has none):** a day past 9999-12-31, a month or a year past 9999, a period or a run past 9999-12-31 (`load/indexes.rs`, `load/spans.rs`, seven sites); a range past u32 or i64 (`load/indexes.rs`, five); a unit past the 64-bit powers (`load/calls.rs`, two); too many keys (`load/rows.rs`); a long postfix chain (`load/types.rs`); at run, a range of more than a million keys (`eval/aggregate.rs`, two) and a calendar with thousands of days off in a row (`eval/builtin.rs`). For the coordinator: one issue for the next spec round, to state the limits and their codes (L §8.3 has `LOAD-DEPTH` for nesting, nothing for these).

**Unreachable by construction (a lookup the namespace guarantees, a form the parser never makes):** "a band / def / input / kind / index / move / place / thing / leg that isn't built" (`load/`, 29 sites: the namespace holds the id, its table not), "a kind whose parent is missing", "a cycle of the prelude's kinds", "an entry no version states" (`load/kinds.rs`), "a range of that period word" (`load/indexes.rs`: the parser takes `year`, `month`, `day`, `int` only), "a type of that word" (`load/decls.rs`: the parser's word list is load's), "a row key of that form" (`load/rows.rs`), "a period out of order" (`load/indexes.rs`, three: a step is at least 1), "a span with no day" (`load/spans.rs`: the parser takes a date or a period only), "a model header without on, start or end" (`load/steps.rs`: `LOAD-HEADER` first), "a dated read with no law date" (`read/rows.rs`: `LOAD-LAWDATE` first). M1, with the user's deferral G-S2 (the interner and id-keyed `Table<I, T>`): the lookups become typed ids, the rest `ENGINE-INVARIANT`.

**Load, not met by any probe (refcore's behaviour for the form unknown):** "a computed key of a move instance" (`load/observed.rs`: `rent[amt]` refuses as refcore does, `LOAD-KEY amt is not a key of flat`), "a keyed place's key that isn't a literal" (`load/places.rs`), "a priced reference with fields" (`load/prices.rs`, two), "a stepped range's key of that kind" (`load/keys.rs`); and in `reprint`, a `same` item whose side is keyed by an expression or is no name (`syntax/canon.rs`, two: forms outside the grammar's `sref`, the second STOP-REPORT's kind). For the coordinator: probe them in M1's first session, and twin each or mark it an invariant.

**Run (S's evaluator; load is M0's), by the milestone that builds it:**
- **M1** (the household forecast): an ask in a scenario's world (`ask/answer.rs`, `ask/worlds.rs`) and a scenario's expression row at run (`eval/read.rs`); an ask's pins, an axis at run, `as if` (`ask/answer.rs`, `eval/expr.rs`); the ask forms beyond values and residuals, a partial answer (`ask/answer.rs`); the written projections `low`, `last(x, d)`, `value`, `windows each` (an ask's and a balance read's), a projection outside a def, a band, a move or an ask (`eval/expr.rs`, `walk/reads.rs`, `walk/projection.rs`); a known window or a moves line at run (`ask/answer.rs`); `prorata` (`eval/expr.rs`); decisions: a written order with a plan, an unordered cycle of decisions due on one day, a decision whose day is a hole, a ties by over values of two kinds, a reading after a decision (`walk/agenda.rs`, `walk/order.rs`, `walk/day.rs`, `book/reading.rs`); a read of a walk while it is walked, a leg's read whose walk needs a cell still being computed (`walk/projection.rs`, `walk/reads.rs`, `eval/rules.rs`); a band over days, periods or keys, a band's `replace` rows that form a whole table or have no table under them (`eval/band.rs`, `read/bands.rs`); keys and bindings beyond S's (`*` in a read's, a place's or a move's keys, a key literal of a key list, a range or an axis, keys after a value, keys of a place, a move or a member, a bare aggregate over a tuple of keys, a binding over a kind with no day or span, over an index with a day or span, a binding whose day or span reads its own variable, whose day is a hole, a member whose lifespan ends at a stated hole, a stepped range of numbers, a range of days with no step, a read with fewer or more keys than binds, a key that isn't an integer, flows of a move at more keys than it binds, flows of a move's field: `eval/`, `walk/`); membership in a value or of a value in a kind, `missing` of a value that isn't an input's read, `pos` of a key the run can't see, `inf` in an expression, a record field that is a hole or an expression, the type of an expression the run can't see without reading, a built-in S's realities don't call, the text of a span's end or an expression in a message (`eval/`, `ask/residuals.rs`, `walk/reads.rs`); the calendar built-ins beyond S's (a calendar written in a rule, one that isn't an input's read, a calendar's added day that is a hole or a window: `eval/`).
- **M2** (replay against reality): `same` lines at run, an attribute naming a movement (`ask/answer.rs`); the value of an observed row, a movement or a window as a row's value (`eval/read.rs`); readings at an instant other than a day's start or end, of a place not named, of a place of unit any (`book/reading.rs`); a version that answers a hole movement (`book/adjust.rs`); a leg of an instance a later version made exist, a move's `fulfils` that isn't the variable of its binding over adjustments (`book/members.rs`, `book/settle.rs`); bindings over movements with no day or span, uncertain movements under `with uncertain`, pads or decisions, a read of a decision's movement (`book/members.rs`); the modelled balance and the ledger in full: residuals by a field, the ledgers of a place of unit any (`walk/reads.rs`, `book/ledger.rs`); a leg booked outside every group of the sign rule (`walk/day.rs`); prices read from records: prices by an index that isn't a def keyed by years (`eval/read.rs`).
- **M4** (law breadth): `first`, `last`, `argmin`, `argmax` (`eval/aggregate.rs`); flows for a period or converted (`walk/reads.rs`: M1 builds `flows` in full, M4 `in CUR at fx`).
- **M5a**: fills at run (`ask/answer.rs`). **M5b**: parts at run (`ask/answer.rs`); a leg's amount of a party or a batch, `bal` of a party or a batch (`eval/expr.rs`, `walk/reads.rs`). **M5c**: a joint model's asks and a member's ask `a.q` at run (`ask/answer.rs`, `ask/worlds.rs`); a binding over a member's index or kind (`eval/aggregate.rs`).
- **M6**: `to u` and the transcendental values, a power that isn't an integer, `solve` (`eval/expr.rs`, `eval/builtin.rs`, `eval/band.rs`, `eval/rules.rs`); `cdf`, `pdf`, `pmf`, `crps`, orient at run.
- **M7**: distributions and random asks at run, queries (mean, share, quantile) (`eval/expr.rs`, `eval/read.rs`, `ask/answer.rs`).
- **None expected:** "a load refusal met at run" (`ask/answer.rs`): a refusal the evaluator meets that load should have made; after M0 it is a load gap, to report as one. "a turn names an ask the reality doesn't hold" (`ask/answer.rs`): an invariant of the turns' order.

## 2026-11-05 M0, G11: the gate's review: B1's ruling, B2 to B6, O1, O2, O4, O5; O3 not reproduced (issue #56)

The M0 gate's review (`tmp/m0/gate-review.md`, part 1) found 6 blocking gaps and 6 other. This step takes B1 to B6 and O1 to O5, in one commit; O6 isn't taken (report-only, R-127, noted for M1 in #90). Each fix was reproduced first, on refcore and the engine (`tmp/m0/g11/p.py`, `o3.py`, `rp.py`; outputs `p-0.txt` before, `p-2.txt` after).

- **B1, the ruling (the coordinator's pick P15, no code):** the symbol table stays keyed by name (`model/reality.rs`, `names: BTreeMap<String, NameKind>`), the sort its value, a joint member's prefix folded into the name (`a·x`): **the prefixed name stands in for (path, name).** L §2.5's one namespace holds as names are inserted (`LOAD-DUP`, `m0_codes`). Seam N's table keyed by (path, sort, name), BUILD-ORDER § M0's and the plan's `model/symbols.rs`, is M1's, built with G-S2's interner and id-keyed `Table<I, T>` (the user's deferral, 2026-11-02; issue #90): a string-keyed table built now would be rebuilt there. This closes the G3 entry's deviation ("lands at G9 with them"): G9 folded the prefix into the name, and this is the ruling for it. The "M0 built" entry said nothing about it; this entry is its correction (no committed line is edited).
- **B2, `lifespan` typed at load** (`load/calls.rs`, refcore's `c_lifespan`): one argument; a bare key naming a thing read as a key of the thing's kind (L §2.5); a key of a kind gives the record `(from date, to date)`; anything else is `LOAD-UNIT lifespan of <t>, not a thing`. It went to `args_only` and was `Unseen`, so `lifespan(a1)`, `lifespan(konto, world)` and `lifespan(konto).zz` loaded. `covered` is the one other name of `BUILTINS` without an arm, and the parser makes it its own expression, never a call, so `args_only` is gone and the fall-through is `ENGINE-INVARIANT`. 15 probes print refcore's bytes (10 differed). **For T: no vector holds `lifespan` at load.**
- **B3, `same` sides** (`syntax/expr.rs`, `syntax/item.rs`, `syntax/canon.rs`): the parser reads a `same` side as L §2.3's `sref`, keys written as literals (refcore's `ref` shape, one key list after the id and after each field, so `x[a][b]` stays refused as refcore refuses it), in a version item and in the header line alike (`same_ref`, sharing `reference`'s walk). `reprint` keys every side form (refcore's `item_key` for `same`): a side ending in keys by them and the text before them, any other side by its written text. `same fee[a1].x`, `same a.b.c`, `same fee[a1].x[a2]` reprinted "not built"; now they print refcore's bytes, the sha256 line included (`tmp/m0/g11/fmt/b.tally`). **Correction to the "M0 built" entry:** its "not met by any probe" line called these two forms "outside the grammar's `sref`", which is wrong for both: they are `sref`, and the two `reprint` stops are gone.
- **B4, a fraction past 64 bits** (`syntax/spacing.rs`): reduced by the number kernel (`Num::checked_div`, then `fraction_parts`, new in `num/text.rs`), no `i64`: `100000000000000000000/30` reprints `10000000000000000000/3`, as refcore (was `ENGINE-INVARIANT`).
- **B5, the reprint's sort** (`syntax/canon_order.rs` `sorted_by`): a stable bottom-up merge sort whose comparisons return `Result` and stop at the first failure, so no sort is handed a comparator that isn't a total order. Reproduced: the old `sort_by` with its `Equal`-after-failure comparator panicked on 21 mixed keys ("user-provided comparison function does not correctly implement a total order", `core/src/slice/sort/shared/smallsort.rs:854`, `tmp/m0/g11/b5-panic.txt`); the merge returns the mismatch's `ENGINE-INVARIANT`.
- **B6, a path count's text** (`load/cone.rs` `int_text`): the `Num`'s integer digits (`integer_text`, new in `num/text.rs`): `path 99999999999999999999` prints `LOAD-PARSE … without paths N seed S` and `LOAD-DOMAIN path … is outside 1..3`, as refcore's `%d` (was `ENGINE-INVARIANT`).
- **O1, a refusal's reach in a field, not its message** (`base/refusal.rs`, `Reach`, `reaching`, `reach`): what a refusal points at beyond its words is `Reach::UnloadedWorld` (an ask naming a world the run doesn't load, refused alone, `load/pins.rs` `unloaded_world`) or `Reach::Id(id)` (the id an `unknown id` or `ambiguous key` names, set by `Stop::naming`, `syntax/parse.rs`, at each of the 42 sites that word one, in `load/` and `eval/`). `read_outside` (`load/worlds.rs`) takes the id from the reach, not from the message's first word (`first_word` gone); `refused_alone` (`load/check.rs`) asks the reach, not the message's text (`UNLOADED` gone). `renamed` drops the reach, its message being another's. No message changes: every probe prints the same bytes before and after (`p-2.txt`, `p-3.txt`), T's 115 stay green.
- **O2, no swallowed stop:** `literal_place` narrows its swallow to the refusals it means (a key no index holds names no literal place; `ENGINE-INVARIANT`, "not built" and depth pass on), so it, `place_target`, `change_of`, `flows_target`, `line_random`, `random_reads`, `seen_labels`, `Cone::add` and `random_cone` return `Result`; `check_table`'s "is it whole" test (`load/bands.rs`) and `batch_of_move` (`load/parts.rs`) match `Err(Stop::Refused(r)) if r.code() != Code::EngineInvariant`, as `load/across.rs` does; `entry_place` and the priced kinds' files (`load/prices.rs`) pass a missing section's file on; `movement_field` (`load/types.rs`) is `leg_type`, its unit's error passed on; `hoisted_sames` (`load/merge.rs`) refuses a member without its model's file (`ENGINE-INVARIANT`, refcore's `KeyError`) and reads the header as refcore's `mls[1:]`; `to_money` (`load/binds.rs`) passes the division's error on. The review's `unwrap_or(Ty::Dyn)` is no swallowed error: an attribute the prelude declares has no written type, refcore's `DYN` (a comment says so now).
- **O3, not reproduced (left as it is):** an arrival rule's day `m.day + 99999999999999999999` never reaches `day_offset`: every figure past ±1 in a rule is `LOAD-LITERAL` first (L §7.1), in refcore and the engine alike (`tmp/m0/g11/o3-0.txt`: `bal(konto, m.day + 99999999999999999999 start)`, `… day start`, `m.day - …`, `value(…)`, each `LOAD-LITERAL figure 99999999999999999999 in a rule; make it a row`). An `i64` sum of terms of ±1 can't overflow.
- **O4, one helper each:** a pin's keys (`load/pins.rs` `pin_keys`, the word `address` or `key`); `instance_text` (`load/same.rs`'s, `moves.rs`'s removed); `cmp_sign`, `agg_word`, `num`, `unit_stop`, `converts`, `int_flex`, `is_duration` (`load/types.rs`'s, the copies in `ops.rs`, `exact.rs`, `calls.rs`, `random.rs` removed); `arity` (`load/calls.rs`'s, `exact.rs`'s removed).
- **O5, the headers:** `load/calls.rs` (no call is typed `Unseen` any more; `c_lifespan` in its twin list), `load/reads.rs` (an `of q` reads the labels the parts clauses give), `load/types.rs` (its `Twin:` line no longer repeats `ops.rs`'s).
- **Deviations (B3, both of #88's family, refcore printing or sorting by its parse tree; the coordinator's pick P12 (a) sends refcore's fix to the next spec round):** (a) a `same` side's key that isn't a literal is `LOAD-PARSE` (`same fee[1 + 1] = fee[a1]`: `LOAD-PARSE cannot parse: expected "]", got +`; `same fee[*] = …`: `… expected a literal, got *`), where refcore's parser takes an expression and load prints its node, `LOAD-KEY bin(op='+', l=num(v=Fraction(1, 1), …)) is not a key of flat`; the "M0 built" entry's #88 form "a `same` side keyed `*`" is now this parse refusal, in the engine's wording until the round words it, and `load/same.rs`'s "a reference keyed by * or a computed key" can't be met. `same fee[-1]` is a literal and prints `LOAD-KEY -1 is not a key of flat`. (b) A side deeper than `a.x` (`a.b.c`, `x[k].f`) sorts by its written text, where refcore's `item_key` sorts it by its node's repr (`field(obj=field(obj=name(id='a'), …`): `same b.x` and `same a.b.c` print in the other order there (L §2.4: "as an item whose target and keys are its left side's"; `tmp/m0/g11/p-2.txt`, `same-deep-order`). Own test: `format_cases.rs`, `a_same_side_s_keys_are_literals_and_a_deeper_side_sorts_by_its_text`.
- **Green ids:** none of T's (M0 gates, 115 of 115 green before and after). Every probe of `p.py` but these two deviations prints refcore's bytes (50 same, 9 differ: the 7 parse refusals and `same-neg` of (a), `same-deep-order` of (b)).
- **Own tests (not gating):** `load_cases.rs`, `lifespan_and_a_path_count_of_any_size_are_checked_at_load_as_refcore_checks_them` (14 `lifespan` forms, 5 path counts, each refcore's line); `format_cases.rs`, `same_sides_of_every_form_and_fractions_of_any_size_reprint_as_refcore` (refcore's reprint and hash) and the deviations' test; `num/text.rs`, `fractions_and_integers_print_their_digits_of_any_size`; `syntax/canon_order.rs`, `a_failing_comparison_stops_the_sort` (B5's keys, 2 to 1 000) and `the_sort_is_stable_and_ordered`.
- **mutants-local** (`-F 'fraction_parts|integer_text' num/text.rs`): 8 mutants, 4 caught (`fraction_parts`), 4 missed (`integer_text`: `None`, `Some("")`, `Some("xyzzy")`, the `!` deleted). **For T:** no vector prints a path count (`paths 0`, `path p` outside `1..N`, `path p` without `paths`), nor a fraction past 64 bits, nor a `same` side of three names or with a field after its keys, nor `lifespan` at load.
- **Size:** 38 files, +952 −488 in engine/crates: the code +719 −488 (B2 +30 and `args_only` −30; B3 +110; B4 −20; B5 +40; B6 +2; `num/text.rs` +20; O1 +145 −108, the `Reach` field and `naming` +50, a line at each of 42 sites; O2 about +150 −90, most of it `Result` through nine functions; O4 about −120 net), the own tests +233. The plan has no G11 row; the coordinator's share is about 1 % of the week. What drove it: O2's chain of callers (a place lookup's error passed through the random cone and the reads), O1's 42 sites, B3's parser and keys.
- **Totals:** `milestone-report: M0` prints no line (M0 gates since 8d34e4c; its 115 tests run in `gating`, green). Exception delta +0 (6; report-only hits 5, as locked). check full: "check full: green, 31 steps (1580s)"; "check fast: green, 12 steps (114s)"; the own tests: "check step test-dev: green, 1 step (68s)".
- **Next:** the coordinator's: the gate's part 2 on this commit. For T: the vectors listed under mutants-local above. Open STOP-REPORTs: none new (B3's two deviations are #88's family, P12 (a); O3 not reproduced; O6 not taken).

## 2026-11-05 M0, G12: the gate's second review: a scenario row's `covered` call, `lifespan`'s citation, a `same` side keyed by a keyed place, leftovers 1 to 4 and 6 to 8 (issue #56)

The gate's second review (`tmp/m0/gate-review.md`, "Second review") found N1 (blocking), N2 and N3, and nine small leftovers; this step takes N1 to N3 and leftovers 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 7 and 8 (5 is #88's, 9 #89's). Each was reproduced first on refcore and the engine (`tmp/m0/g12/p.py`, `arms.py`, `n1t.py`; outputs `p-0.txt` before, `p-2.txt` after).

- **N1, `household.covered(…)` in a scenario's expression row** (`load/calls.rs`, `load/reads.rs` `covered_call`): reproduced with the reviewer's input (T's `codes/m0/scen`, `place konto` added, `replace amt = household.covered(konto, 2027-01-01)`): the engine printed `ENGINE-INVARIANT a built-in with no typing arm: covered`, refcore `LOAD-PARSE cannot parse: covered(x, span)`. `covered_call` twins `m_c_covered` on a call: two arguments (else `… takes two arguments`), the first a place (`place_arg`: `LOAD-UNIT covered of money PLN, which is not a place`, `LOAD-KEY zz is not a key of places`), then `LOAD-PARSE cannot parse: covered(x, span)`, a call's argument never being a span. **The other built-ins, listed against refcore** (`arms.py`): refcore's `Checker` has 46 `c_<name>` arms, the load checker's list held the same 46 names, and each but `covered` had its arm; refcore's other 29 names of `parser.BUILTINS` (the aggregates, the queries, `bal`, `flows`, the distributions…) have no arm and are looked up (`household.sum(amt)`: `LOAD-NAME unknown id sum`), as the engine did. The 46-name list is gone: the match's arms are the list, and its `_` arm is refcore's lookup (`no_builtin`: `unknown id`, `is not called`), so no fall-through is left to be an invariant. 7 `covered` forms and 7 other names print refcore's bytes. **Corrections to the G11 entry:** its B2 sentence ("the parser makes it its own expression, never a call, so … the fall-through is `ENGINE-INVARIANT`") was wrong for a scenario row, where a base prefix makes `covered` a call; and `calls.rs`'s `Invariants:` line, which said the same, now says the lookup.
- **N2, `lifespan`'s citation:** L §5.7 (`lifespan(k)`), §2.5 (a bare key), in its doc and the header's `Spec:` line (was L §4.2, §5.3).
- **N3, a `same` side keyed by a keyed place** (`syntax/expr.rs` `lit_key`, `syntax/canon.rs`): reproduced: refcore loads and reprints `same lim[konto[ana]] = lim[konto[ben]]` and `same opening[konto[ana]] = …`; the engine refused them at parse since G11 (`expected "]", got [`). Twinned: an `sref` key is a literal or a place keyed by literals (L §2.3 `rk`'s last form, one key by L §2.4), read as the reference `konto[ana]`, which load's `KeyLit::of_expr` resolves as a place key (`load/same.rs`'s `KeyLit::Place`, reachable again); `reprint` keys it as a row's place key (`KeyRef::Place`). Load and reprint print refcore's bytes, the sha256 line included; a run over a `same` of rows stops "not built" whatever its keys (M1's, as before). **Deviation, #88's family:** `same lim[amt[ana]] = …` is `LOAD-KEY amt[...] is not a key of accounts`, where refcore prints its node's kind, `LOAD-KEY index is not a key of accounts`; a computed key inside, `fee[konto[1 + 1]]`, stays `LOAD-PARSE`.
- **Leftover 1:** `round`'s flex arm passes the division's error on (`map_err(num_stop)?`, as `load/binds.rs`).
- **Leftover 2, `a.adjusts`:** `movement_field` (`load/types.rs`) reads a kind's attributes by name, as refcore's `kind_attrs`: a prelude attribute has no written type and is `dyn`, an adjustment's `adjusts` a key of `events` (refcore's one arm). `argmax(… adjustments …).adjusts` now types `key events` (was `Unseen`), `.change` `dyn` (`LOAD-UNIT rule base of ls is dyn, not date`, as refcore; it loaded). The comment the review found wrong is gone. Not taken: the rest of `_ => Unseen`, refcore's kind-test narrowing, its reading by name over `events` and its `LOAD-FIELD` (`… .adjusts.zz` loads, refcore says `LOAD-FIELD zz is not a field of events`): several arms and the scope's narrowing, for #90.
- **Leftover 3, `lifespan` and `Unseen`:** the pass is gone, as refcore's `c_lifespan` has none; probed first for a valid argument load types `Unseen` (an observed row, `argmax` over a kind, `a.adjusts`): each loads in both, so none is refused by it. `lifespan(a.change)` is `LOAD-UNIT lifespan of dyn, not a thing`, as refcore (it loaded). `model/ty.rs`'s `Unseen` doc says what it now covers.
- **Leftover 4, wrong in part:** `load/same.rs`'s `input_side` stop can't be reached (a `same` side's keys are literals and place keys) and is `ENGINE-INVARIANT`. `move_instance_keys`' stop **is reached**: an observation's attribute of type `key wages` naming `wage[*]` or `wage[1 + 1]` prints "not implemented" (exit 69), where refcore prints `LOAD-KEY None is not a key of months` (its `None`: #88's family). It stays "not built", with a comment saying so.
- **Leftover 6, the headers:** `num/text.rs` (`Spec:` L §2.4, §8.3; `Twin:` `parser.py`'s `lit`, its fraction's canon, and a count's `%d`), `syntax/expr.rs` (`sref`), `load/calls.rs` (above). `ARCHITECTURE.md` is the lead's.
- **Leftover 7:** `AggFn::word` (`syntax/tree.rs`) replaces `agg_word` in `load/types.rs` and `eval/aggregate.rs`.
- **Leftover 8, the guards name their code:** `literal_place` (`load/check.rs`) and `batch_of_move` (`load/parts.rs`) take `LOAD-KEY` as no place or batch, the one refusal `resolve_key` and `keyed_place_thing` make; `check_table`'s whole test (`load/bands.rs`) takes `LOAD-BAND` as "not whole". Any other stop is passed on (a number past the cap in `check_table`, an invariant).
- **Green ids:** none of T's (M0 gates; `gating` green). Probes (`p-2.txt`): 36 of `p.py`'s print refcore's bytes; the 10 that differ are the run (`n1-base`, `n1-abs`, `n3-run`, `n3-lim-run`: a scenario's or a `same`'s run, not built), the deviation above (`n3-amt-run`), leftover 4's (`l4-*`: `*` and `1 + 1` the "not built" stop against refcore's `None`; `konto[ana]` the engine's `placekey` against refcore's `None`, as before G12; `2026-03` an attribute naming a move's instance, not built, where refcore says `LOAD-KEY wage[2026-03] is not a key of wages`) and leftover 2's `LOAD-FIELD` not taken (`l3-adjusts-x`). G11's probes print as before (`tmp/m0/g11/p-4.txt` = `p-3.txt`).
- **Own tests (not gating):** `load_cases.rs`, `a_built_in_called_through_a_scenario_row_s_base_prefix_is_checked_as_refcore_checks_it` (8 rows, refcore's lines for the test's own texts, `n1t.py`), and 5 more forms in G11's `lifespan` test (`.change`, `.adjusts`, `lifespan` of each, `.adjusts.day`); `format_cases.rs`, `a_same_side_keyed_by_a_keyed_place_reprints_as_refcore` (refcore's reprint and hash, and the computed key's refusal).
- **For T, forms no vector holds:** N1's, a built-in called through a scenario row's base prefix (`household.covered(…)` in each of its four refusals, `household.sum(x)`); N3's, a `same` side keyed by a keyed place (load and reprint); an adjustment's `.adjusts` and `.change` typed at load; `lifespan` of `dyn`.
- **mutants-local:** not run: no kernel function changed (`num/text.rs`'s header only).
- **Size:** 16 files, +292 −158 in engine/crates: the code +181 −158 (N1 +40 and the 46-name list −50; N3 +45; leftover 2 +12; leftover 7 +19 −30; the rest headers and guards), the own tests +111. The coordinator's budget for G12 is about 0.5 % of the week. What drove it: N1's list of arms against refcore's, and the probes leftovers 3 and 4 needed before changing a stop.
- **Totals:** exception delta +0 (6; report-only hits 5, as locked). check full: "check full: green, 31 steps (541s)"; "check fast: green, 12 steps (67s)"; the own tests: "check step test-dev: green, 1 step (44s)".
- **Next:** the coordinator's. Open STOP-REPORTs: none new (N3's `amt[...]` and leftover 4's are #88's family; leftover 2's narrowing and `LOAD-FIELD` to #90).

## 2026-11-05 M0, G12 (2 of 2): a field of an adjustment's adjusts is checked at load (issue #56)

T's STOP-REPORT (`engine/tests/GAPS.md`, 9bd4247; the held fixture `adjust/adjusts-field`, `codes/m0/held.tsv`) and the coordinator's pick P19 (a): the rest of the second review's leftover 2, before the tag.

- **Reproduced** (`tmp/m0/g12/p.py`, `l3-adjusts-x`, the held fixture's edit of `codes/m0/adjust/base.tally`): `def ls: date = argmax(1 for a in adjustments over ..).adjusts.zz` loaded on the engine (`REQ-ASK no ask zz`), where refcore refuses `LOAD-FIELD zz is not a field of events (base.tally:29)`; its twins `.adjusts` (`LOAD-UNIT … key events, not date`) and `.adjusts.day` (loads) agreed.
- **The fix** (`load/types.rs` `movement_field`, `event_field`, `may_narrow`): after a movement's legs, attributes, `day` and `fulfils`, refcore's `m_movement_field` narrows by a kind test, then reads a field of `events` by name over every event kind, then refuses `LOAD-FIELD <field> is not a field of <kind>`. Its narrowing reads only a field whose object is a name (parentheses aside), and load keeps no kind test's narrowing, so such a field stays `Unseen` as before; any other object (`….adjusts.zz`, `argmax(…).zz`, `(…).zz`, a field after keys) takes refcore's two steps: over `events`, the first event kind in the order declared (the kinds' table keeps refcore's insertion order) with a leg of that name gives a leg's record of unit `any` (`(from key places, to key places, amount dyn, day date)`), with an attribute its declared type or `dyn`; else `LOAD-FIELD`.
- **Probes:** 12 forms (`f-*`, `l3-adjusts-x`): 9 differed before (`f-0.txt`), 1 after (`f-1.txt`): `f-bound`, `argmax(1 for a in adjustments over .. where a.zz)`, a field of a bound name, `Unseen` where refcore, with no kind test narrowing `a`, refuses `LOAD-FIELD zz is not a field of adjustments`: the narrowing, not built, stays #90's. The twins print as before; every G12 probe else as before (`p-3.txt`: 50 same, the 10 that differ the 9 of G12's entry, `l3-adjusts-x` now matching, and `f-bound`); G11's as before (`tmp/m0/g11/p-5.txt`).
- **Green ids:** none of T's (M0 gates; `adjust/adjusts-field` is held, for T to move to `edits.tsv`). It prints refcore's bytes.
- **Own tests (not gating):** 3 rows in `load_cases.rs`'s `lifespan` test (`.adjusts.zz`, `.adjusts.cash`, `argmax(…).zz`, refcore's lines, `t-adjusts-zz`, `t-adjusts-cash`, `t-zz`).
- **For T, no vector holds:** a leg or an attribute read by name through `.adjusts` (`.adjusts.cash`, `.adjusts.change`), and `LOAD-FIELD` of a movement of a declared kind read through an aggregate (`argmax(1 for w in wages over ..).zz`).
- **mutants-local:** not run: no kernel function changed.
- **Size:** 2 files, +73 −9: the code +56 −9, the own tests +17. Budget about 0.2 % of the week.
- **Totals:** exception delta +0 (6; report-only hits 5, as locked). check full: "check full: green, 31 steps (536s)"; "check fast: green, 12 steps (80s)"; the own tests: "check step test-dev: green, 1 step (41s)".
- **Next:** the coordinator's. Open STOP-REPORTs: none new (a bound name's field before a kind test narrows it, #90).

## 2026-11-05 M0, G13: the gate's third review: lifespan of a field load doesn't see, a unit or a count of axis combinations past the cap (issue #56)

The gate's third review (`tmp/m0/gate-review.md`, "Third review"; pick P21) found G1, S1 and S2, and five leftovers, of which the third rides along; the others are #90's. Each was reproduced first on refcore and the engine (`tmp/m0/g12/p.py`, `g1-*`, `s1-*`, `t-g1-*`; `tmp/m0/g12/s2.py`).

- **G1, `lifespan` of a field load types `Unseen`** (`load/calls.rs`): G12's leftover 3 removed `lifespan`'s `Unseen` pass, which was wrong (the gate's advice, its own finding says). Reproduced with the reviewer's input (`adjust/base.tally`, `kind gifts: event certain` / `leg cash: PLN` / `attr giver: key accounts`, `def early: int = count(e in events over .. where lifespan(e.giver).from < e.day)`): refcore loads it, the engine refused `LOAD-UNIT lifespan of unseen, not a thing`; the same under a kind test (`where e in gifts and …`). The pass is back: `Unseen` is a type refcore knows there and load doesn't see yet (a bound name's field, P20), not "no type"; `dyn` is refused still (T's `adjust/lifespan-dyn`, `g1-dyn`). Both forms load. **Corrections:** G12's entry said `lifespan` refuses `Unseen` and the probes showed no valid argument load types `Unseen`; both were wrong, as a bound name's field shows. `model/ty.rs`'s `Unseen` doc, `load/types.rs`'s `Invariants:` line and `load/reads.rs`'s (`Dyn` is refused where refcore refuses `DYN`, a place argument) now say what holds.

Each was reproduced first on refcore and the debug binary (`tmp/spec-2.1.6-build/rp.py -split`; the review's probes copied to `tmp/spec-2.1.6-build/p37/`, the sweep's in `p37/sweep/`, written by `gen_sweep.py`). The rows: `d = 2026-06-01`, `imin = -9223372036854775808`, `imax = 9223372036854775807`, `huge = 100000000000000000000`, `n1 = 1`, `cal = calendar(weekends [sat, sun])`, `cal7 = calendar(weekends [mon, tue, wed, thu, fri, sat, sun])`, `calh` an input with no row; `top` 4 096 nines, `ntop = -top`.

- **G1, `add_business` past 64 bits reads the calendar first** (`eval/builtin.rs`): the count is an exact integer; past 64 bits it outnumbers the calendar's days, so the walk reads the calendar day by day until it meets 0001-01-01 or 9999-12-31, as refcore's `count`. `add_business(d, huge, calh)`, `add_business(d, -huge, calh)` are `?"calendar calh doesn't cover 2026" (calh)` (were `RUN-LIMIT`); `add_business(d, huge, cal7)`, `(d, -huge, cal7)` `RUN-DOMAIN calendar cal7 has no business day` (were `RUN-LIMIT`). Twins at `imax`: the same.
- **G2, a duration past 64 bits that isn't a whole number of the period's periods** (`eval/arith.rs` `period_shift`, `periods_in` on exact integers): the whole-number check comes before the limit, as refcore's `date_arith`. `year_of(d) + huge * 1 month` is `RUN-DOMAIN 2026 + 100 000 000 000 000 000 000 month: not a whole number of its periods (L §4.2)`, `quarter_of(d) + huge * 1 month` `RUN-DOMAIN 2026-Q2 + 100 000 000 000 000 000 000 month: …` (were `RUN-LIMIT`).
- **G3, a range whose ends are near the 4 096-digit cap** (`num/rational.rs` `Num::range_keys`, new; `eval/aggregate.rs` `range_values`): the keys are counted on the big tier, uncapped, as refcore's Python integers, and the walk never computes the key after the last, so every key it makes lies between the ends, under the cap. `sum(n1 for k in top .. top)` `1`; `sum(n1 for k in top - n1 .. top)` `2`; `sum(n1 for k in n0 .. top step top)` `2`; `sum(n1 for k in ntop .. top step top)` `3`; `sum(n1 for k in n0 .. n5 step s4091)`, `… step s4095` (steps of 4 091 and 4 095 digits) `1`; `sum(n1 for k in ntop .. top)` `RUN-LIMIT -9 999 … .. 9 999 … holds more than 1 000 000 keys` (all were `RUN-DIGITS`). Twins: `sum(n1 for k in n0 .. a7 step s7), sum(n1 for k in c7 .. mm1 step s7)` `(1 000 000, 1 000 000)`, `sum(n1 for k in n0 .. b7 step s7)` `RUN-LIMIT 0 .. 7 000 000 step 7 holds more than 1 000 000 keys` (`rg/r20`, `r21`, as before).
- **G4, a list key moved past 64 bits** (`eval/arith.rs` `key_shift` on exact integers): `index li = [a, b, c]`, `def z[k in li] = if pos(k) > n1 then w[k - huge] else n2`: `z[b]` is `RUN-KEY b - 100000000000000000000 is outside li`, `w[k + huge]` at `a` `RUN-KEY a + 100000000000000000000 is outside li` (were `RUN-DOMAIN outside its operation's domain`). Twins (`ky/k5`, `k7`, an integer index) as before.
- **G5, `day_of` and `schedule` past 64 bits** (`eval/builtin.rs`, through `whole_of`): `day_of(month_of(d), huge)` is `2026-06-30`, `schedule(month_of(d), huge, following, cal)` `none` (were `RUN-DOMAIN`); twins at `imax` the same; `day_of(month_of(d), -huge)` `RUN-DOMAIN day_of(m, -100000000000000000000): a day number below 1`.
- **G6, the texts** (`eval/arith.rs`): messages with a garbled "lhs" where refcore says "a" or names the value. `month_of(d) + h5 * 1 month` (`h5 = 2.5`) is `RUN-NOT-INT 2.5 month is not an integer` (was `… lhs duration that is not an integer`); `year_of(d) + n1 * 1 month` `RUN-DOMAIN 2026 + 1 month: not a whole number of its periods (L §4.2)`; `a comparison of two records has no meaning (compare their fields)` (refcore's `evaluate.py:2343`); `day is a dimension of its own` and `a rate times a duration of another period dimension`, two `LOAD-UNIT`s met at run, which print "not implemented", so their bytes don't show. No other "lhs" text is left in the crates.
- **The sweep** (`eval/*.rs`: every `int_of`, `to_i64`, `to_u64` and `try_from` of a value; 26 sites listed in `tmp/spec-2.1.6-build/sweep-sites.txt`). Internal counts and positions (an aggregate's count, a key's position, a member count) take no user integer. A four-digit year (`written_year`, a year read) can't pass 64 bits. Checked with a probe past 64 bits and **fixed beyond G1 to G6:**
  - `power` (`eval/builtin.rs`, for `periodic` and `factor`): a whole power past 64 bits keeps 1, 0 and -1 (any other base passes the cap, `RUN-DIGITS`, as before). `factor(rz, huge, year, effective)`, `factor(rz, huge, month, nominal 12)`, `factor(rz, -huge, year, effective)` (`rz = 0 %/year`) are `1` (were `RUN-DIGITS`).
  - a `nominal` compounding past 64 bits (`eval/expr.rs` a literal, `eval/read.rs` a row): refcore takes it and refuses the power at run, `LOAD-TO periodic needs to u`, a load refusal met at run, which the engine stops "not implemented" (`answer.rs`'s `load_at_run`). `periodic(r5, month, nominal 100000000000000000000)` was `ENGINE-INVARIANT a compounding code past the integers`, and as a row `LOAD-UNIT a value of the wrong type`; both now print "not implemented", as `nominal 9223372036854775807` already did.
  - a calendar's `from` year past 9999 (`eval/read.rs`, `time/calendar.rs` `FirstYear`): the year its rule compares is 10000, after every day there is, and it prints as written. `cy = calendar(weekends [sat, sun], yearly [01-01] from 100000000000000000000)`: `is_business(d, cy)` is `true` (was `LOAD-UNIT a calendar's first year`).
  - **Not fixed:** `pos(k + huge)` for a key of an integer index stops "not implemented" at any size (`pos(k + n1)` too, refcore `RUN-KEY 4 is outside ks`): not built, for the follow-up issue. `factor(r5, huge, year, effective)` and `factor(r5, imax, year, simple)` (`r5 = 5 %/year`): the engine says `RUN-DIGITS a value passes 4 096 digits; …` (L §7.1); refcore runs past the probe's 120 s, so there are no bytes to compare, and this is not a STOP-REPORT (the spec is clear).
- **Probes:** the second review's 75 asks print refcore's bytes; the 7 that differ are out of scope (`rg/r6`, `r10`, `r13`, a `sum` over an empty range; `kl/q1`, `q2`, G9; `lit/l1`, `l2`, the literal range held for #97). The 4 missed are the guard's `&&` → `||` (lines 168) and `||` → `&&` (169): no caller passes a fraction or a step not positive (`range_values` takes whole ends and refuses such a step first, `RUN-DOMAIN`), so no input reaches the guard; the own test kills each, which T's runner, the mutation set, doesn't run. ## STOP-REPORT 2026-11-06: refcore moves a key of a year range `by n` (n above 1) 12 months a period, off its index's keys, where L §4.2 moves it by whole keys

- **Kind:** the spec seems right and refcore wrong (no vector holds the form)
- **Where:** refcore `evaluate.py:2133-2151` (`date_arith`: it checks the duration against the key's size, `amount % a.n`, and takes `b = Int(amount // a.n)` periods; then its year branch starts the result at `add_months(a.start, 12 * k)`, one year a period, where its month branch uses `a.shift(k)`, `k * n` months, `dates.py:182`); L §4.1 l.564 ("A key of a range `by n` stands for the n periods from it to the next key"), §4.2 l.626 ("or by a duration that is a whole number of them (`y + 5 years` is `y + 5` …)"); engine `crates/tally/src/eval/arith.rs` `period_shift` (one `Period::shift` for every kind).
- **Rule or gate:** DONE gate 10 (refcore's bytes); L §4.1, §4.2.
- **What happens:** `tmp/spec-2.1.6-build/p38/tw.tally`: `index y2 = year 2026..2040 by 2`, `def f1[q in y2]: date = start_of(q + n2 * 1 year)`, `n2 = 2`, `ask tf1: f1[2026]`. `python3 -m refcore run tw.tally --ask tf1` gives `tf1: 2027-01-01`, exit 0: the period [2027-01-01, 2029-01-01), not a key of `y2`. The same refcore refuses `q + 1 year` there (`RUN-DOMAIN 2026 + 1 year: not a whole number of its periods`), so its own period is two years, and it gives `q + n1` `2028-01-01`; on a month range `by 2` it gives `q + n2 * 1 month` `2026-03-01`, the next key. By L §4.2, `q + 2 years` is `q + 1`, `2028-01-01`, which the engine prints now. The same for `q + n24 * 1 month` (engine `2028-01-01`, refcore `2027-01-01`), `q - n2 * 1 year` at 2028 (`2026-01-01`, refcore `2027-01-01`) and the anchored `index fy2 = year 2026..2040 by 2 from 04-06`, `q + n2 * 1 year` (`2028-04-06`, refcore `2027-04-06`). No reading of the spec gives refcore's: one where a year key's own period is a year takes `q + 1 year`, which refcore refuses.
- **What I tried:** nothing beyond the spec's reading, which the fix of H1 gives with no code of its own (the engine moves every period by its step, as refcore's month branch does). Printing refcore's bytes would need a branch for year keys that leaves the index's keys.
- **What I ask:** options: **(a) Recommended:** the next spec round fixes refcore's year branch to move by `a.shift(k)` when `a.n` isn't 12, as its month branch does; no spec text change; T holds the four asks for the issue. **(b)** the spec says a year key `by n` moves one year a period (then `q + 1 year` should be taken, which no text says). **(c)** the engine prints refcore's bytes until the round.
- **State:** last green commit bd7aa4a (T's 5c7a2d1 on it); this step's commit holds the spec's values.

## 2026-11-07 pre-M1, G1a: load's limits and #88's forms (issues #97, #89, #88, #90)

The builder of the pre-M1 run, group G1a of `tmp/pre-m1-build/plan.md` (the coordinator's P11, the builder's B-8), on T's a763b3b, 0b4beea and bbe4601: the 18 `Logged-Red:` ids marked G1a. Each vector was reproduced first on the debug binary against `codes/m0.refcore` (`tmp/pre-m1-build/g1a/fx.py`), and its edges probed on refcore and the engine, one edit of a fixture base each (`g1a/pr.py`, `p1.tsv` to `p7.tsv`).

- **Unit powers (L §7.6 item 4):** `model/unit.rs`: `UnitError` (a number error, or the base whose power passes 64); every written power is checked first, in the order written, then each summed power in the order its base first appears (refcore's `make` and its dict), in `from_syntax`, `mul`, `div`, `power` and `subst`; `make` keeps its number error for the callers that can't pass the limit (a base to the first power, a unit's halves), so the kernel's `walk/plan.rs` and `book/reading.rs` are unchanged. `LOAD-LIMIT unit <u> has a power past 64` at the line load meets it (the checker's scope; a declaration's, a row's or an observed leg's line); at run, the same load refusal (a unit a key gives, `eval/state.rs` `unit_stop`). **B-8:** a written power is a whole number of any size (`syntax::UnitTerm.power` a `Num`), so the parser no longer refuses "a power too large" and the refusal comes in load's order; `pow`'s literal power is any size too. Probes, refcore's bytes (15): `PLN^64` loads to its type, `PLN^65`, `1 PLN^64 * 1 PLN`, `1 PLN^64 / 1 PLN^-1`, `PLN^99999999999999999999`, `1/PLN^-9223372036854775808`, `pow(1 PLN, 65)`, `pow(1 PLN, ±100000000000000000000)` (was `LOAD-UNIT … not a literal`), `pow(1 PLN^2, 32)`, `year^40*month^40` (`unit month`), `%^64`, `input y: num PLN^40*PLN^40`.
- **A move's seen day (item 1):** `load/across.rs` `check_seen_day_limits`: a move whose day load sees, folded at every key of its bindings; the end of time read as a day, or the fold's `RUN-LIMIT`, is `LOAD-LIMIT move <m>[<keys>] on <e>: <message>` at the move's line, the moves in the reality's order (refcore's `m_check_seen_day_limits`). Probes: `end_of(y)` over `year 9998..9999` (`late[9999]`), two bindings (`late[9999, a]`); `end_of(y) - 1 day` loads.

The builder of the pre-M1 run, group G1b of `tmp/pre-m1-build/plan.md` (the coordinator's P8, C1 (a), and P11), on G1a's 197ed36: the four `Logged-Red:` ids T marked G1b.
